# Plugins · T · part 3

1,346 entries, tenantext to the-blessed-handoff. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **tenantext**: A SessionStart hook injects the Simplified English (adapted ASD-STE001) output rules at session start and after /clear, resume and compaction. A PreToolUse hook denies the shell commands of the kit command rules. /ste toggles the language default. /claude-mode selects Default, Auto or YOLO for future tenant-claude launches only. /spawn_agent starts a Herdr agent pane. /handoff_coordinator hands the work to a new coordinator pane and closes the previous one. After build-skills.py runs in the kit clone, the plugin also carries the skills that the kit manifest declares for tenantext. ([ryg40/tenant-pi](https://github.com/ryg40/tenant-pi/tree/HEAD/claude-code))

- **terminator**: Stop hooks that terminate a Claude Code session when the agent's final message contains a kill phrase. Two scripts: single-kill (ends Claude) and double-kill (ends Claude plus the terminal shell that launched it), each bound to its own phrase. Installs at local (project) or global (user) scope; scripts require LOCAL or GLOBAL arg and exit 1 if missing. Contains-match (robust to model preambles), case-sensitivity configurable, and a kill path that walks the process tree to the owning claude (and, for double-kill, the top-most shell ancestor) and never signals pid &lt;= 2.
